Upcoming seats · this window

Full-window math is exact (deterministic pool). Paying every spin yourself averages ~0.86× — profit only comes from windows whose cumulative return turns positive before a stop, and from index advances that are free (other players / free spins).

Lock a position on the Profit tab to list every seat ahead.

How to read this. Each bet size has its own cursor into the same 60,000-row pool. The table starts from the last-known DB snapshot cursor for each denomination and projects the best-exit net inside the horizon.

The cursor has only advanced if spins were played since the snapshot — confirm your chosen denomination with your own observed spins before committing, then switch to the Profit tab.

RULE // 01

What you enter (exactly)

1. Game — the title on the screen.
2. Bet — the denomination you are playing. Same bet for the whole lock session (each denomination has its own cursor).
3. One entry per spin, in order, no gaps — a win is the cash amount shown, a loss is LOSS (0.00).

Free / Win-A-Spin rounds pay at $0.25 and advance the $0.25 cursor — enter them in a $0.25 session, not this one.

Undo removes the last entry; Reset starts over.

RULE // 02

How profitability works here

Every spin pays multiplier[cursor] × bet, and the cursor advances +1 per spin (wraps 60,000→1). The pool is a fixed 60,000-row table. So once the cursor is pinned, the exact stake / return / net / ROI of the next N spins is computable — no randomness, no estimate.

Best exit = the spin inside the horizon whose cumulative net is maximal. That is the seat you play to, then stop. If no spin puts cumulative net into the black, the app says SKIP — wait for the cursor to drift to a warmer window.

Capital, not crest: the path to a crest dips before it pays. Capital locked is the worst dip of that ride's cumulative net — the cash you must survive with before you ever reach the seat. Enter it in "Capital to lock"; if a profitable exit's path dips deeper, the app says BLOCK / UNAFFORDABLE and refuses to ride it. This is why a "$0.25 stake never busts" reasoning is wrong: the stake is cheap, but the round trip to the crest is what can drain you to zero if you don't have the capital to survive the path.

The estimate that matters: the pool averages 0.86, so playing every spin yourself loses ~14% of what you stake. A positive window only pays if the index reaches it — free advances (other players, or free spins) are what make that realistic. Never chase to "recover".

RULE // 03

Seats & Scan tabs

Seats — every multiplier ≥ your threshold on the Profit tab (default ×5), tagged JACKPOT (≥×500) / PRIZE (≥×100) / BIG, with cost-to-reach and net-at-stop.

Scan — starts from the last-known DB snapshot cursor of all ten denominations on the current game and ranks them by best projected exit. The cursor moves as spins are played, so the snapshot is a starting point, not the truth.
